Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11880 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A plate-loaded T-bar row with a close grip for thick back work.

Proposed staging

Stand on the platform with your chest supported. Grab the close-grip handle with straight arms.

  2. Grab the close-grip handle with straight arms. 11880.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the handle toward your torso, elbows close. 11880.json#/instructions/2

  5. Lower slowly to full extension. 11880.json#/instructions/4

1189 — weighted v crunch (on bosu ball)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1189 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A V-shaped crunch on a bosu ball with added weight, which challenges the abs and balance together.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bosu ball and lean back slightly, holding a weight in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1189.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1189.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a bosu ball and lean back slightly, holding a weight in front of you. 1189.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your feet off the floor with your legs bent or straight. 1189.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bring your chest and knees toward each other in a V shape. 1189.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower back with control, keeping your spine long. 1189.json#/instructions/3
  5. Stay stable on the ball throughout. 1189.json#/instructions/4

11903 — standing hip flexors stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11903 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing stretch for the front of the hip and thigh, useful after long periods of sitting.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand tall and hold a wall or support for balance.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11903.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 11903.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 11903.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Wall surface. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 11903.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand tall and hold a wall or support for balance. 11903.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend one knee and bring your heel toward your glute, holding the ankle. 11903.json#/instructions/1
  3. Tuck your pelvis slightly and push your hips gently forward. 11903.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your knees close together and your chest upright. 11903.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds, then switch legs. 11903.json#/instructions/4

11904 — standing half kneeling hip flexors stretch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11904 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

A half-kneeling position that opens the front of the hip and the quad of the back leg.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Kneel on one knee with the other foot forward and a pad under your knee.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11904.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the torso until the source's arm action starts; preserve documented load, handle or support contacts.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 11904.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11904.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kneeling/support pad or cushion. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 11904.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Kneel on one knee with the other foot forward and a pad under your knee. 11904.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your torso upright and tuck your pelvis under. 11904.json#/instructions/1
  3. Shift your weight forward until you feel a stretch at the front of the back hip. 11904.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your front knee over your ankle and your core engaged. 11904.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds, then switch sides. 11904.json#/instructions/4

  7. Press the dumbbells overhead, fully extending your arms. 1201.json#/instructions/6
  8. Lower the dumbbells back to your shoulders and repeat the entire sequence for the desired number of repetitions. 1201.json#/instructions/7

A single-arm fly on an incline bench that targets the upper chest. Working one side at a time helps you spot and fix left-right differences.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the incline bench to a 30-45 degree angle. Sit on the bench with a dumbbell in one hand, resting it on your thigh. Lie back on the bench, keeping your feet flat on the ground. Hold the dumbbell with your arm extended straight up over your chest.

A one-arm chest fly with your upper back on a stability ball. The wobbly base brings your core in while the chest does the work.

Proposed staging

  6. Engage your chest muscles to bring the dumbbell back up to the starting position. 1286.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch arms. 1286.json#/instructions/6

1290 — dumbbell one arm press on exercise ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1290 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A single-arm dumbbell press done seated or lying on a stability ball. Balance and core control are part of every rep.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an exercise ball with your feet flat on the ground and your knees bent at a 90-degree angle. Hold a dumbbell in one hand and position it at shoulder height, with your elbow bent and palm facing forward.
